# Lazy launchers Task 5: invariants, `--dynamic` rows, final gate (index 0)

Benchmark gate for Task 5 (the last task) of `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-lazy-dynamic-launchers.md`:
the dynamic-value invariant tests, `bench_launch.py --dynamic`, docs/TODO, the dead
`if (!intern)` branch, and a trial of 64-byte alignment for the hot entry functions (rejected, below).
Task 5 changes no hot-path instruction: the only runtime edit deletes a never-taken branch in the
cold interner call. Baseline: `2026-09-27_lazy-baseline_0_f507cfc.md`; Task 4: `2026-09-28_lazy-task4_0_7223147.md`.

## Environment

- Candidate: `8d95e66` (branch `develop`), clean checkout, for the gate, the rerun, the baseline A/B and `--dynamic`.
- Baseline: `f507cfc` (`/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-baseline`). The baseline A/B tree is `/tmp/intj-lazy-base`, a detached worktree of
  `24bf5d1`, which is f507cfc's code plus its journal.
- Alignment A/B trees: `/tmp/intj-lazy-t4` (`f4454e9`, Task 4 before 4b), `/tmp/intj-lazy-t5noalign` (`8d95e66`), and the
  main checkout at `8d95e66` plus an **uncommitted** diff (sha256 below). Each file's `commit=` line says which.
- CPU: `taskset -c 0`, one process at a time. Shared host; load averages (1/5/15 min):
  `lazy-task5` 8.13, 8.75, 7.77 -> 3.29, 5.77, 6.76; `lazy-task5-rerun` 4.54, 6.66, 7.03 -> 3.23, 5.23, 6.42; baseline A/B 3.23, 5.23, 6.42 -> 3.59, 5.13, 6.34.
  The alignment A/B files log `/proc/loadavg` per process. `top` showed the container 98% idle; the load comes from other tenants.
- GPU: AMD Instinct MI308X (`gfx942`), GPU 0 (`HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`); tests ran on GPUs 1,2.
- Python 3.12.3 (`/tmp/gb2/bin/python`), Torch `2.14.0+rocm7.2`, Triton `3.8.0`, gcc/cc 13.3.0.
- `INTJ_BENCHMARK_ROOT=/tmp/gbench`. CUDA: untested (no NVIDIA GPU).

## Commands

```bash
HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 /tmp/intj-bench/run_all.sh lazy-task5
/tmp/gb2/bin/python /tmp/intj-bench/bench_compare.py lazy-baseline lazy-task5
HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 /tmp/intj-bench/run_all.sh lazy-task5-rerun   # rows flagged, rerun once
/tmp/gb2/bin/python /tmp/intj-bench/bench_compare.py lazy-baseline lazy-task5-rerun
HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 bash /tmp/intj-bench/lazy_ab.sh lazy-task5     # 5 interleaved rounds, baseline vs HEAD, --no-gpu
/tmp/gb2/bin/python /tmp/intj-bench/lazy_ab_summary.py /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-ab lazy-task5
for r in 0 1 2; do PYTHONPATH=$PWD HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 taskset -c 0 /tmp/gb2/bin/python benchmarks/bench_launch.py \
  --dynamic --iters 100000 --batches 9 > /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5/extra_round${r}_launch_dynamic.txt 2>&1; done
bash /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-ab.sh        # alignment trial 1: intj_call + intj_bound_entry
bash /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-call-ab.sh   # alignment trial 2: intj_call only
/tmp/gb2/bin/python /tmp/intj-bench/lazy_ab_t5_align_summary.py /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-ab
/tmp/gb2/bin/python /tmp/intj-bench/lazy_ab_t5_align_summary.py /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-call-ab
```

Every one of the 60 gate files has `exit_status=0` (60/60).

## Result

**No regression attributable to Task 5, and the plan's acceptance holds.** In the 5-round interleaved A/B against the
plan's starting commit, every `--no-gpu` row of an untuned launcher without dynamic values is between 3.0 ns faster and
0.4 ns faster. The spec asked for "within 1 ns of today".

Rows flagged in **both** runs are the costs Tasks 2 and 3 already accepted, at the values those tasks recorded:

| row | baseline | lazy-task5 | rerun | Task 4 (run / rerun) | why |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| ffi_paths INTJ cold compile callback + cache | 404.6 | 706.2 | 699.0 | 708.7 / 707.3 | Task 2's accepted per-launcher miss callback |
| launch_readme decode ns, static_compile | 89.7 | 96.9 | 94.9 | 96.3 / 94.1 | Task 2's accepted `spec_key(launcher, device, *args)` row |
| launch_readme decode ns, runtime_shim | 91.1 | 94.4 | 93.9 | 95.7 / 94.9 | same |
| launch_last_key 32 alternate | 103.3 | 112.3 | 113.0 | 112.1 / 112.6 | Task 3's code placement (trial below) |
| launch_last_key 32 repeat | 99.0 | 106.0 | 106.6 | 106.7 / 106.1 | same |
| launch_sweep 32 int | 105.9 | 113.3 | 113.1 | 113.9 / 113.3 | same |

Flagged in one run only, so not regressions under the gate rule:
- `launch_gpu verify on`: +4.4% in the first run, +0.5% in the rerun. This is GPU submission noise.
- `launch_readme grid=(1,) intj`: +0.0% in the first run, +19.2% in the rerun. Same cause.
- `ffi_paths INTJ pair prebuilt *tuple`: +2.3% (3.1 ns) in the first run, then +3.4% (4.7 ns) in the rerun, just over the threshold.
  This is not Task 5: in the two interleaved alignment A/Bs, `f4454e9` against `8d95e66` reads 137.0 / 136.4 and 137.2 / 136.0.

| row | base ns | lazy-task5 ns | delta |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| auto map | 42.7 | 41.3 | -1.4 |
| baked | 39.1 | 38.6 | -0.5 |
| bound pointer | 44.5 | 41.5 | -3.0 |
| bound tensor | 45.6 | 42.7 | -2.9 |
| fixed device map | 42.5 | 41.4 | -1.1 |
| fixed device no-map | 44.0 | 43.0 | -1.0 |
| reduced key | 41.6 | 41.2 | -0.4 |
| verify off | 42.3 | 41.2 | -1.1 |
| verify on | 43.3 | 42.2 | -1.1 |

### `--dynamic` (3 separate processes, GPU 0, 100000 calls × 9 batches; not gated, no baseline)

| path | round 0 | round 1 | round 2 | median |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| lazy plain | 3025.3 | 3087.8 | 3516.0 | 3087.8 |
| 2 dynamic options | 3175.7 | 3075.6 | 3079.2 | 3079.2 |
| str constexpr | 2974.3 | 3000.6 | 2986.1 | 2986.1 |

These rows launch a real kernel. The ~3 us `hipModuleLaunchKernel` call and its
process-to-process spread (up to 0.5 us) are orders of magnitude larger than the host cost the plan
expected to see: 2-4 ns for two dynamic decodes, 1-3 ns for a `str` pointer compare. As written,
the mode cannot resolve those ns-level costs. It only shows that the dynamic and `str` launchers
work and are not slower at the microsecond scale.

Fix round 1 added a host-only `spec_key` column and an `int` constexpr row, which resolve these costs.
Two dynamic options cost +7..+10 ns over plain, and a `str` constexpr costs -0.3..+1.4 ns over an
`int` one. The runs, and the 12-case suite that now includes `--tuned` and `--dynamic`, are in
`2026-09-28_lazy-task5-final_0_9868517.md`.

### 64-byte alignment of the hot entry functions: rejected

Criterion (the Task 3 ruling and the Task 5 dispatch): adopt the alignment if it is net-neutral or
better across rows. That means no row gets worse beyond noise, and the placement-sensitive rows
(last_key 32, sweep 32, adapter kwargs) become stable. "Beyond noise" here means a delta larger
than the max-min spread of that row's 5 per-round medians on either side.

Trial 1 aligns both functions (`INTJ_HOT_ALIGN`, `__attribute__((aligned(64)))`, in
`intj_map.h`). `nm` confirmed 64-byte alignment in both the C and the C++ (STATIC_COMPILE) builds.
Trial 2 aligns `intj_call` only. Each trial ran 5 interleaved rounds in separate processes on
core 0, across `f4454e9`, `8d95e66` and the aligned working tree.

Trial 1 (both functions), against `8d95e66`:
- Gains on the rare 32-argument shapes:
  - `last_key 32 alternate`: -9.6 ns;
  - `last_key 32 repeat`: -7.5 ns;
  - `sweep 32 int`: -8.5 ns.
- One clean loss, on a common shape: `bound tensor` +1.5 ns, with spread 0.5 on both sides.
- Other rows are slower, but inside their run's spread, so these are not established losses:
  - `sweep 32 tensor` +1.6 (spread 2.3);
  - FFI wrapper positional +2.0 (spread 3.4);
  - `pair manual unpack` +6.1 (spread 8.6);
  - `pair prebuilt *tuple` +2.7 (spread 6.3).

Trial 2 (`intj_call` only):
- Smaller gains: -2.0 / -4.6 / -3.2 ns.
- Clean losses:
  - `bound tensor` +2.0 (spread 1.7 / 0.6);
  - `fixed device no-map` +1.4 (spread 0.8 / 1.0).
- FFI wrapper kwargs +2.6 is inside its spread (1.9 / 3.0).

**Decision: reverted, not committed.** Both trials trade a clean ~1.5-2 ns loss on `bound tensor`
(a common launcher shape) for gains on 32-argument launches (a rare shape), so neither is net-neutral
across rows. The 32-argument placement loss stays as Task 3 accepted it. `TODO.md` lists a
hot/cold section split as the upgrade path.

Uncommitted trial diffs (applied to `8d95e66`):
- `lazy-task5-align-both.diff`, sha256 `473c5d449b23a96f5c22b9bd88dcd69c80c6318b77cc6aefcb20ab88c5f0b65c`;
- `lazy-task5-align-call.diff`, sha256 `2b006473b892603860a0fe3ae581385603c3cac3a83a0ceb0955e85f173c8528`.

## Timing caveats

The host is shared (load averages above). GPU submission rows vary by about 20% between processes.
Host rows are pinned to core 0 but share L3 with other tenants. The A/Bs interleave trees within
each round to cancel drift.

## Raw outputs

Local artifact paths:
- `/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5/` and `/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-rerun/`: `round<0-2>_<case>.txt`, 30 files each, plus `DONE`. `lazy-task5/` also holds `extra_round<0-2>_launch_dynamic.txt`.
- `/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-ab/`: `<base|lazy-task5>_r<0-4>_launch_host.txt`.
- `/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-ab/` and `/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-call-ab/`: `<t4|noalign|align>_r<0-4>_<case>.txt`.

### lazy_ab.sh

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# /tmp/intj-bench/lazy_ab.sh <label> -- 5 interleaved rounds: baseline worktree vs HEAD, host matrix
set -u
label=$1; out=/tmp/intj-bench/$label-ab; mkdir -p $out
repo=/mnt/nvme2/jinpli/workspace/home/jinpli/development/intj
base=/tmp/intj-lazy-base
[ -d $base ] || git -C $repo worktree add --detach $base "$(cat /tmp/intj-bench/lazy-baseline/COMMIT)"
for r in 0 1 2 3 4; do
  for tree in base:$base $label:$repo; do
    name=${tree%%:*}; dir=${tree#*:}
    (cd $dir; echo "commit=$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
     PYTHONPATH=$dir taskset -c 0 /tmp/gb2/bin/python benchmarks/bench_launch.py --no-gpu --iters 100000 --batches 9 2>&1
    ) > $out/${name}_r${r}_launch_host.txt
  done
done
echo done > $out/DONE
```

### lazy-task5-align-ab.sh

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# 5 interleaved rounds, separate processes, core 0: f4454e9 vs 8d95e66 (Task 5, unaligned) vs working tree (+INTJ_HOT_ALIGN)
set -u
out=/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-ab; mkdir -p $out
repo=/mnt/nvme2/jinpli/workspace/home/jinpli/development/intj
PY=/tmp/gb2/bin/python
for r in 0 1 2 3 4; do
  for tree in t4:/tmp/intj-lazy-t4 noalign:/tmp/intj-lazy-t5noalign align:$repo; do
    name=${tree%%:*}; dir=${tree#*:}
    for c in "launch_host|benchmarks/bench_launch.py --no-gpu --iters 100000 --batches 9" \
             "launch_sweep|benchmarks/bench_launch.py --sweep --iters 100000 --batches 9" \
             "launch_last_key|benchmarks/bench_launch.py --last-key --iters 100000 --batches 9" \
             "ffi_paths|benchmarks/bench_intj_ffi_paths.py --mode all --iters 1000 --batches 9"; do
      case=${c%%|*}; args=${c#*|}
      (cd $dir; echo "commit=$(git rev-parse HEAD)$( [ $name = align ] && echo ' +INTJ_HOT_ALIGN (uncommitted)')"; echo "load=$(cat /proc/loadavg)"
       PYTHONPATH=$dir INTJ_BENCHMARK_ROOT=/tmp/gbench taskset -c 0 $PY $args 2>&1
      ) > $out/${name}_r${r}_${case}.txt
    done
  done
done
echo done > $out/DONE
```

### lazy-task5-align-call-ab.sh

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# 5 interleaved rounds, separate processes, core 0: f4454e9 vs 8d95e66 (Task 5, unaligned) vs working tree (+INTJ_HOT_ALIGN)
set -u
out=/tmp/intj-bench/lazy-task5-align-call-ab; mkdir -p $out
repo=/mnt/nvme2/jinpli/workspace/home/jinpli/development/intj
PY=/tmp/gb2/bin/python
for r in 0 1 2 3 4; do
  for tree in t4:/tmp/intj-lazy-t4 noalign:/tmp/intj-lazy-t5noalign align:$repo; do
    name=${tree%%:*}; dir=${tree#*:}
    for c in "launch_host|benchmarks/bench_launch.py --no-gpu --iters 100000 --batches 9" \
             "launch_sweep|benchmarks/bench_launch.py --sweep --iters 100000 --batches 9" \
             "launch_last_key|benchmarks/bench_launch.py --last-key --iters 100000 --batches 9" \
             "ffi_paths|benchmarks/bench_intj_ffi_paths.py --mode all --iters 1000 --batches 9"; do
      case=${c%%|*}; args=${c#*|}
      (cd $dir; echo "commit=$(git rev-parse HEAD)$( [ $name = align ] && echo ' +INTJ_HOT_ALIGN intj_call only (uncommitted)')"; echo "load=$(cat /proc/loadavg)"
       PYTHONPATH=$dir INTJ_BENCHMARK_ROOT=/tmp/gbench taskset -c 0 $PY $args 2>&1
      ) > $out/${name}_r${r}_${case}.txt
    done
  done
done
echo done > $out/DONE
```

### lazy-task5-align-both.diff

```diff
diff --git a/intj/runtime/entry.c.jinja b/intj/runtime/entry.c.jinja
index 81cc9ad..00ff147 100644
--- a/intj/runtime/entry.c.jinja
+++ b/intj/runtime/entry.c.jinja
@@ -1132,7 +1132,7 @@ static INTJ_ALWAYS_INLINE int intj_parse_call(intj_state *st,
 }
 
 /* args starts at grid controls, then dynamic values, then the public kernel arguments. */
-static PyObject *intj_call(intj_state *st,
+INTJ_HOT_ALIGN static PyObject *intj_call(intj_state *st,
                            int64_t device_ordinal, uint64_t stream,
                            PyObject *const *args, Py_ssize_t nargs,
                            intj_bound_launcher *bound) {
@@ -1299,8 +1299,9 @@ static PyObject *intj_call(intj_state *st,
 }
 
 {% set guarded = fixed_device or bound_params or grid_py_mode or tuned %}
-static PyObject *intj_bound_entry(PyObject *self, PyObject *const *args,
-                                 Py_ssize_t nargs) {
+INTJ_HOT_ALIGN static PyObject *intj_bound_entry(PyObject *self,
+                                                PyObject *const *args,
+                                                Py_ssize_t nargs) {
   intj_bound_launcher *bound = (intj_bound_launcher *)self;
   intj_state *st = (intj_state *)bound->state;
   if (INTJ_UNLIKELY(!st)) {
diff --git a/intj/runtime/intj_map.h b/intj/runtime/intj_map.h
index 526ce0f..2f1f593 100644
--- a/intj/runtime/intj_map.h
+++ b/intj/runtime/intj_map.h
@@ -21,8 +21,12 @@
  * whether a parameter is usually a tensor or an int. */
 #define INTJ_LIKELY(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 1)
 #define INTJ_UNLIKELY(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 0)
+/* The launch hit path's own functions start on a cache line, so growing cold
+ * code ahead of them cannot shift their placement (measured 4-10 ns). */
+#define INTJ_HOT_ALIGN __attribute__((aligned(64)))
 #else
 #define INTJ_ALWAYS_INLINE inline
+#define INTJ_HOT_ALIGN
 #define INTJ_LIKELY(x) (x)
 #define INTJ_UNLIKELY(x) (x)
 #endif
```

```diff
+++ b/intj/runtime/intj_map.h
@@ -21,8 +21,12 @@
  * whether a parameter is usually a tensor or an int. */
 #define INTJ_LIKELY(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 1)
 #define INTJ_UNLIKELY(x) __builtin_expect(!!(x), 0)
+/* The launch hit path's own functions start on a cache line, so growing cold
+ * code ahead of them cannot shift their placement (measured 4-10 ns). */
+#define INTJ_HOT_ALIGN __attribute__((aligned(64)))
 #else
 #define INTJ_ALWAYS_INLINE inline
+#define INTJ_HOT_ALIGN
 #define INTJ_LIKELY(x) (x)
 #define INTJ_UNLIKELY(x) (x)
 #endif
```
